# Product Image Sources

Every image used for a product, product range or category on this site, with where it
came from and what kind of image it is. Images are downloaded and served locally - nothing
is hotlinked - and are resized and re-encoded to WebP. Nothing about what an image shows
is altered; the only editing is cropping page furniture off a catalogue cover.

**Image types**

| Type | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `exact-image` | The product's own photograph, published by its own approved source. |
| `manufacturer-image` | The manufacturer's own photograph of that product range, used where the source publishes no photograph against the individual size. |
| `representative-image` | A correct-product-type photograph, used only where neither of the above exists. Always labelled as such on the product page. |

**Permission and licensing should be confirmed with each supplier before production use.**
Supplier photography remains the supplier's own. Star Electric Enterprises claims no
ownership of it and no dealership, distribution or authorisation relationship with any
brand shown. Representative images are openly licensed (Creative Commons); the licence and
author for each are given in the notes below and in `assets/image-sources.md`.

Compiled 2026-09-07.

| Families / series carrying an image | 179 of 181 |
